- `baseline_mid_turns_avg_gar`: 0.01219

Wait — this is confusing. The `mid_turns_avg_gar` for the BASE model is 0.01068 (ours) and 0.00303 (dongre). The `baseline_mid_turns_avg_gar` is 0.01219 for both.


Ah, I see it now — since this is the base model evaluation with no checkpoint and gate_type=none, the `mid_turns_avg_gar` values are the actual baseline measurements, while `baseline_mid_turns_avg_gar` appears to reference something else entirely. Looking back at the v7 eval data, that 0.0122 baseline was the base model's mid-turn GAR, which matches what I'm seeing here.

So comparing across datasets: the base model's mid-turn GAR on UltraChat (0.01068 for ours, 0.00303 for dongre) is actually lower than what was measured on standard_conversations (0.01219), which is interesting because it suggests the model performs differently depending on the conversation dataset.

Now I need to pull the full per-turn GAR arrays for both variants to see how performance varies across individual turns.

_source line 3609 · 2026-05-19T06:58:44.802Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have all the data I need. Let me compile the key findings:

**Base model (Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct) GAR on UltraChat (50 conversations, 4 turns each):**

| Metric | GAR-ours | GAR-Dongre |
|--------|----------|------------|
| Turn 1 | 0.0494 | 0.0122 |
| Turn 2 | 0.0200 | 0.0051 |
| Turn 3 | 0.0122 | 0.0033 |


| Turn 4 | 0.0091 | 0.0028 |
| mid_turns_avg | 0.01068 | 0.00303 |
| baseline_mid | 0.01219 | 0.01219 |

My approach shows a dramatic degradation across turns—dropping 81.6% from turn 1 to turn 4, with the steepest decline happening between turns 1 and 2. The Dongre baseline stays consistently lower throughout, and my method's average across middle turns exceeds the baseline while theirs falls well below it.

The key difference is methodological: my GAR measures attention from a single response position to the goal, whereas Dongre's approach averages across all assistant tokens, which naturally produces lower values since it's distributing attention across more positions.

The UltraChat dataset reveals the core phenomenon more dramatically—an 81.6% GAR decay over 4 turns in the base model—which is stronger evidence than what we see in the standard_conversations set. Looking at the comparison across datasets, the UltraChat baseline is notably lower than standard_conversations, which suggests the datasets have different characteristics that affect how attention degrades.

I should verify whether v7's improvements were also tested on UltraChat, since that would give us a clearer picture of the recovery effect across both datasets. The base model measurements here fill an important gap—we now have GAR data on a larger, more representative sample that strengthens the paper's core argument. Since this was a pure base model run with no training involved, I don't need to register it formally, but I should make sure these results are properly tracked before posting the review.
```

The model weights download is incomplete. There's a `model.safetensors.index.json` (the shard index file) but **no actual .safetensors weight files**. The index file tells you which shards to load, but the shards themselves (~16GB total) are missing.

This means the download only retrieved metadata files (config.json, generation_config.json, model.safetensors.index.json, LICENSE, README) but not the actual model weights.

Total: 11MB — only config + tokenizer + metadata. The model weights (.safetensors) would be ~16GB in 4 shards typically.


The task marked itself complete even though the actual weight files never downloaded, which is a serious issue since the model can't run without them. This could've happened if the ModelScope download timed out, if the command was restricted to certain file patterns, or if there was a disk space problem. Let me check what's happening with the disk.

_source line 3687 · 2026-05-19T07:22:23.470Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
LoRA-only training is now running successfully. Key observations:

1. **Gate type = none**: All gates fixed at 1.000 across all layers ✅
2. **Trainable params**: 5,046,272 (0.07%) — LoRA only, no gate params ✅
3. **LoRA params: 112, Gate params: 0** — correct ✅
4. **GPU**: 85% util, 88943/97887 MiB — active and running ✅
5. **Step 0**: L_NTP=1.3786, L_GAR=0.2294 — GAR loss is nonzero despite no gate!


6. **Step 50**: L_NTP=1.6638, L_GAR=0.2344 — The GAR loss is barely budging, which tells me LoRA alone is struggling to increase attention redistribution without an explicit gating mechanism to guide it.

The GAR loss at initialization (~0.23) comes from the base model's low GAR (~0.02), and the loss is trying to push LoRA to reshape attention patterns to increase it. But without gates to directly control which heads redistribute attention, LoRA has to work indirectly through weight adjustments, which appears to be inefficient.

Comparing to v7, which had gating mechanisms that could more directly scale residuals and affect GAR, this LoRA-only approach seems fundamentally limited. The key question is whether GAR will eventually decrease as training progresses, or if LoRA simply can't move it without explicit gating control.
```

</details>

- `/root/.cache/huggingface/`: **4.9GB** — HF cache (another copy of Qwen + ultrachat dataset)
- `/root/turn_residual_gating/runs/`: 248MB — all checkpoints

Total cache: ~27GB out of 30GB total disk. The Qwen model likely has duplicates in both modelscope and huggingface cache.


So I'm looking at the math: 30GB total minus the 22GB modelscope cache, 4.9GB huggingface cache, and 248MB of runs leaves only about 2.8GB free, which matches what the system is reporting. To load Llama-3.1-8B I'd need at least 16GB available, so I could delete the HF copy of Qwen to free up 4.9GB, but that still wouldn't be enough—I'd need to remove the modelscope copy instead, which would free up around 15GB, though I'm concerned because the Qwen model is actively being used for the LoRA experiments running right now.

Let me trace through the code to see which copy is actually being loaded. The config points to the modelscope path, so that's the one in use, which means the HuggingFace cache might be redundant. Looking at the actual sizes, the HF cache has about 2.1GB for Qwen while the modelscope version takes up most of that 22GB total, so there's definitely some duplication happening here. Adding this up: modelscope is taking 22GB total, HuggingFace is around 4.9GB, and the runs directory is 248MB, which puts me at roughly 27.1GB used out of 30GB available. That leaves only 2.3GB free, but I need at least 14GB of space to fit the Llama-3.1 model.

Looking at what I can safely remove: the HuggingFace Qwen cache at 551MB isn't being used since the code points to modelscope, and the ultrachat data at 1.6GB is already extracted elsewhere. The modelscope Qwen model itself at 15GB has to stay since it's actively running the LoRA training. Even clearing out all the HuggingFace cache would only give me 7.2GB free, which still isn't enough. The fundamental issue is that this 30GB disk simply can't hold both the Qwen model and Llama-3.1 at the same time.

I need to decide between running experiments sequentially (which prevents direct comparison), using external storage, getting more disk space, or switching to a quantized Llama variant. Found it! There's a `._____temp` directory with 8.6GB of data — this is a **partial/failed ModelScope download** of the Llama-3.1 model that was interrupted (likely due to running out of disk space). This is garbage that can be safely deleted.

If we delete:
1. `._____temp/` (8.6GB) — failed download temp files
2. HF Qwen cache (551MB) — code uses modelscope path
3. HF ultrachat cache (1.6GB) — data already extracted to disk


4. HF hub other files (~2.7GB) — leaves just the essential Qwen and ultrachat caches

That gets us to about 13GB of freed space, but we're still short by roughly 3GB for the full Llama-3.1 model weights. I should also check if there are duplicate Qwen tokenizer files across ModelScope and Hugging Face that could be consolidated.

Even if I clear everything except the active ModelScope Qwen model, the math doesn't work out — a 30GB disk can't comfortably hold two 15-16GB models plus system overhead. The quickest win is deleting that 8.6GB temp directory to recover wasted space, though it won't fully solve the Llama-3 problem.
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
LoRA-only training progress:

- **Step 3500 (Epoch 1)**, gs~1400/3000 → **~47% complete**
- **GPU**: 85% util, 91321 MiB — healthy ✅
- **Gate**: All 1.000 throughout — correct for LoRA-only ✅
- **L_GAR**: 0.0000-0.0073, averaging near 0 — **remarkably similar to v7 and UG at this stage**

Key observation: **L_GAR has collapsed to near-zero in LoRA-only too**. 
 
This convergence pattern is striking across all three approaches — v7 with ThreadGate, uniform gates, and now LoRA-only all show L_GAR collapsing to near-zero, suggesting the MSE target loss is either driving mean_GAR toward 0.5 or the gradient signal is vanishing entirely.

The key insight is that LoRA alone achieves the training GAR target without any gating mechanism, which aligns with v7's observation that training GAR hovered around 0.48 regardless of the gate design. This means the gates aren't actually helping optimize training metrics — the real test will be whether inference GAR shows any meaningful improvement, though v7 already hinted at a massive 30x gap between training and inference performance that gates couldn't bridge.

So this is shaping up as a clean negative result for the gating approach: all three variants will likely converge on similar training GAR around 0.5, but the fundamental train-eval disconnect remains unsolved. I'm at 47% through the run with about 3 hours left, so I'll check back when it finishes.
```

1. **UG description correction**: The ROADMAP now says "per-layer independent gates without turn conditioning" — this is the **correct** interpretation (fixing the earlier "all layers get same gate value" misstatement in the registry). ✅


2. **LoRA-only training observations**: The L_GAR ≈ 0 signal at gs1400 isn't flagged in the ROADMAP, but this is crucial — it's a strong indicator that the three-way ablation will likely converge to similar results.
3. **Llama-3 feasibility**: The ROADMAP notes needing ~16GB after clearing Qwen cache for Llama-3.1, but I only have 15GB free and Qwen itself takes up 15GB for ongoing work. The sequential deletion approach (remove Qwen, then download Llama) is implied but should be made explicit.
4. **Success probability for Route A**: The 20-30% estimate seems optimistic given that LoRA-only shows the same L_GAR collapse as the full variants, all three exhibit identical training dynamics with NTP dominating, and the core problem is the train-eval distribution mismatch rather than an architectural flaw. The two-stage decoupling might resolve NTP-GAR competition but won't address the 30x generation shift, so even if gates learn correctly in Stage 1, they may not generalize to generation mode.
5. **Timeline pressure**: With EMNLP due in ~10 weeks, I need to finish LoRA-only work in about 3 hours, then move through the remaining four-way ablation.

6. **Gaps in the roadmap**: Missing the base model GAR results on UltraChat, and no mention of re-evaluating v7 on standard_conversations for a fair comparison—these would strengthen the analysis. The roadmap itself is solid and well-organized, though the Llama-3 disk space note was slightly misleading and the LoRA-only preliminary signals should be included.
```

</details>

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
This is a systematic review request from the Director about the implications of LoRA-only training signals for the route decision. Let me analyze each question carefully.

**Question 1: Does L_GAR≈0 in LoRA-only sufficiently prove gating contributes nothing during training?**

L_GAR = (mean_GAR - target)². If L_GAR ≈ 0, then mean_GAR ≈ 0.5 (the target). This happens for all three variants:
- TG v7: L_GAR ≈ 0 after convergence (training GAR = 0.48)


- UG: L_GAR ≈ 0 after convergence
- LoRA-only: L_GAR ≈ 0 at gs1400

So LoRA alone achieves the same training GAR target as the gated variants, but the underlying mechanisms differ—in TG and UG, both LoRA and gates reshape the forward pass, while LoRA-only relies solely on attention pattern modifications. Yet all three converge to the same endpoint.

The real question is whether this pattern holds at inference. Gates showed minimal benefit during training, but they might still matter when the model runs on held-out data. To know for sure, I need to see what LoRA-only inference GAR looks like—if it matches TG's weak performance, then gates truly don't help anywhere. If it's worse, that would suggest gates do provide some inference benefit despite being redundant during training.

If LoRA-only inference GAR stays weak like TG, that fundamentally undermines Route A's premise. The two-stage approach assumes NTP-GAR competition is the bottleneck, but if gates contribute nothing to inference recovery regardless of training procedure, then the real problem is the train-eval distribution shift itself—something no training reorganization can fix. That would collapse Route A's viability from 20-30% down to near zero.

There's a slim possibility that gates trained without NTP pressure in Stage 1 could learn genuinely useful patterns (like selective layer attenuation at later turns) that the NTP-constrained gates in v7 never discovered. But the LoRA-only result would strongly suggest the entire gating mechanism is fundamentally misaligned with the problem, making this escape hatch unlikely.

Now I'm evaluating Route B's core contributions and whether the findings can support them.

The work establishes the first systematic architectural intervention for multi-turn GAR decay with a novel problem formulation, plus delivers a comprehensive negative result with mechanistic insights—the reverse turn conditioning finding, the NTP-auxiliary loss competition, the 30x train-eval disconnect, and clean ablations isolating the failure modes. I'm also recognizing that GAR itself becomes a useful diagnostic metric for multi-turn analysis, and the consistent low gates at L3/L4 suggest inherent layer importance differences worth noting.

For an EMNLP Findings submission, the novelty is strong since this is the first attempt at this specific problem with this approach, the experimental design is sound with proper ablations, but the significance is moderate given the negative results.

The key to making this work is framing the mechanistic analysis as a genuine discovery about how LLMs process multi-turn conversations rather than just documenting a failure. I need to validate that the GAR decay phenomenon generalizes across models, not just Qwen, and provide clear guidance on what might actually work. The ablation needs to cleanly explain why each component fails, and the presentation has to emphasize the insights gained rather than the method that didn't succeed.

Now I'm thinking about additional evaluation metrics beyond GAR recovery—specifically looking at whether gated models have worse perplexity or other quality degradation. better language modeling? If TG has worse perplexity than LoRA-only, gates are actively harmful. If similar, gates are neutral. I should also measure how gate values change across turns for both models—TG should show turn-dependent variation while UG shouldn't, which would tell me whether gating is actually responding to the right signals. Looking at layer-specific contributions could reveal which layers benefit most from gating, and I'll want to check attention entropy too since dispersed attention might indicate weaker goal tracking. Beyond that, generation quality on something like MT-Bench would show if gating helps downstream task performance, and I should validate results across different evaluation datasets.

The real test though is plotting the GAR curve across turns for all four models side by side. If the three trained variants all cluster together above the base model, gating isn't making a meaningful difference. But if there's a clear ordering—TG performing best, then UG, then LoRA-only—that would show gating actually provides incremental benefits.
```

</details>

{
  "text": "[severity: warning] LoRA-only 训练信号对路线决策的深度分析\n\n## 1. L_GAR≈0 是否已充分说明 gating 训练贡献为零？\n\n**结论：训练端已充分，但推理端仍需 eval 确认。**\n\nL_GAR = (mean_GAR - 0.5)²。三者都趋零意味着 LoRA 单独就能将训练 GAR 推到 target 0.5。机制差异（TG/UG 通过 gates+LoRA 两条路径，LoRA-only 仅通过 LoRA）不改变终态——LoRA 对 attention pattern 的修改能力已足够满足 GAR loss。\n\n**训练端结论可提前锁定**：gating 机制在训练优化路径上是冗余的。\n\n但推理端存在一个理论上的反例：gates 可能在训练时冗余，但在推理时通过改变 generation-mode 的 residual flow 产生差异化效果。v7 数据（+0.35pp）暗示这个效果极微弱，但需要 LoRA-only eval 做最终排除。如果 LoRA-only 推理 GAR ≈ TG ≈ +0.3pp，则 gates 在训练+推理两端均贡献为零。如果 LoRA-only 推理 GAR = 0pp 而 TG = +0.35pp，则 gates 有微弱但真实的推理端效果。\n\n**建议**：不要基于训练信号提前关闭 Route A。等 eval，但预期管理应偏向 Route B。\n\n## 2. LoRA-only 推理 GAR ≈ TG 对 Route A 的影响\n\n**如果确认 LoRA-only eval ≈ TG eval（都极弱），Route A 可行性降至接近零。**\n\n原因链：\n- Two-stage 的核心假设是 \"NTP 梯度竞争压制了 gates 的有效学习\"\n- 但如果无 gates 的 LoRA-only 达到相同推理 GAR，说明问题不在 NTP-gate 竞争，而在**更深层**：teacher-forcing 下的 GAR 优化根本无法迁移到 generation 模式\n- Stage 1（gate-only + GAR loss，无 NTP）即使学到 \"正确\" 的 gate pattern，Stage 2 的 NTP fine-tuning 仍面临同样的分布偏移\n- 根本矛盾：训练时 attention 看到的是真实 prefix（teacher-forcing），推理时看到的是模型自己生成的 prefix——GAR 信号在两个分布间不可迁移\n\n**成功概率修正**：从 20-30% 降至 **<5%**。仅在 LoRA-only eval 显著差于 TG 时才值得尝试。\n\n## 3. Route B story 强度与 Findings 可行性\n\n**评估：Findings (3.0) 可行性 ~65-75%，需要跨模型验证加持。**\n\n**核心 contributions（按强度排序）**：\n\n(a) **GAR 衰减现象的系统性量化** — Phase 1 证明 70-82% GAR decay，50 conversations 统计基础，这本身是有价值的经验发现\n\n(b) **Residual gating 干预的完整负面结果 + 三路消融** — 首次尝试，干净的实验设计。三路消融清楚归因：turn conditioning 无效、gating 无效、LoRA 主导\n\n(c) **机制分析三大发现**：\n- 反向 turn conditioning（gates 在需要干预时反而退到 identity）\n- NTP-auxiliary 梯度竞争导致 gate 退化的具体机制\n- 30x train-eval GAR disconnect（teacher-forcing 下 GAR 可优化但不迁移）\n\n(d) **层级特异性** — L3/L4 跨 TG/UG 一致低门控，暗示 transformer 层功能分化\n\n**Findings 达标条件**：\n- ✅ 新颖性：无先例工作\n- ✅ 实验严谨性：消融完整、seed 固定、baseline 公平\n- ⚠️ 需要补充：跨模型验证（至少 Llama-3 Phase 1 GAR 诊断，证明衰减现象非 Qwen 特异）\n- ⚠️ 写作关键：framing 必须是 \"发现了 X 机制\" 而非 \"方法 X 失败了\"\n\n**Main track (3.5+) 需额外条件**：跨模型完整训练+消融 或 提出有效的替代方案并初步验证。仅负面结果很难 main track。\n\n## 4. 四路 eval 中可区分 gating 贡献的额外 metrics\n\n**核心 metrics（必做）**：\n| Metric | 测什么 | 区分力 |\n|--------|--------|--------|\n| 推理 GAR curve (per turn) | 四模型叠图，Base/TG/UG/LoRA-only | **最高** — 直接回答 gating 是否有推理效果 |\n| NTP perplexity | 各模型在 held-out 数据上的 PPL | 高 — 如果 TG PPL > LoRA-only PPL，gates 有害 |\n| Gate dynamic range (TG vs UG) | TG gates 跨 turn 的方差 vs UG gates | 中 — 量化 turn conditioning 的实际动态范围 |\n\n**补充 metrics（如果时间允许）**：\n| Metric | 测什么 | 区分力 |\n|--------|--------|--------|\n| Per-layer GAR attribution | 逐层 attention 对 GAR 的贡献 | 中 — 揭示 gates 影响了哪些层的 attention |\n| UltraChat eval（50 convs） | 更大样本的推理 GAR | 中 — 统计更稳健，与 standard_conversations 交叉验证 |\n| Attention entropy per turn | 各层 attention 分布的集中/分散度 | 低-中 — 辅助解释 GAR 变化的注意力机制 |\n\n**最具判别力的单一可视化**：四模型 GAR curve 叠图（x=turn, y=GAR, 四条线）。如果 TG/UG/LoRA-only 三条线重叠且仅微幅高于 Base，gating 贡献为零的结论一目了然，也是论文核心图之一。",

_source line 3849 · 2026-05-19T10:44:06.809Z_

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Good — the eval data file exists (446KB, 2737 lines). It's sampled from ultrachat_8k (index 4201+), not the standard_conversations.json.

One important consideration: the previous v7 eval was done on `standard_conversations.json` (5 handcrafted conversations). This new 4-way eval uses `ultrachat_eval_sample.json` (from training data). This is actually BETTER for the paper — using held-out training data samples provides:
1. More conversations for statistical robustness
2. More realistic evaluation (same distribution as training data)


But wait — I'm noticing a potential issue here. The eval data comes from ultrachat_8k starting at index 4201, which is also the training source. With only 8000 conversations in the training set, there's a real risk of train/eval overlap or at least distribution contamination. That said, since GAR measures attention patterns rather than generation quality, this contamination is less problematic than it would be for accuracy metrics. Still, it's something to flag.

The real puzzle is whether `eval_phase2_gar.py` actually generates text or just measures attention on teacher-forced tokens. From what I saw earlier, it uses teacher-forcing with the full conversation already tokenized, so it's measuring attention patterns on known tokens — not testing generalization. That means using training data here would just replicate what we already know (GAR around 0.5 for all models), which wouldn't reveal the train/eval disconnect. Yet the v7 eval showed mid_turns_avg_gar at 0.0157 with a 30x difference, which contradicts this expectation.

Let me think through the actual difference between training and eval more carefully. During training, the GAR loss is differentiable and flows back through the LoRA gradients, shaping the attention patterns. At eval time, the model runs forward without that gradient signal, so even though both use teacher-forcing, the attention patterns might diverge from what the training loss optimized for. That could explain the 30x disconnect — the eval is measuring the model's actual attention behavior without the training-time optimization pressure.

But wait, if I'm using the same input data at eval time, the patterns should be similar. The real issue might be that the eval data (standard_conversations) is fundamentally different from the training data (ultrachat_8k), causing a domain shift. If I evaluate on ultrachat_eval_sample instead, I might see much higher GAR scores. For the 4-way comparison though, the key point is that all models get evaluated on identical data, so the relative differences should still be meaningful.
```

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_015cZ1VJ7syKps6hDoj6c94G`</summary>

```text
1	# Route B: Negative Result / Analysis Paper 实验设计
2	
3	> 前提条件: LoRA-only eval 确认 gating 贡献为零（LoRA-only ≈ TG），或路线 A two-stage 失败后无缝转入
4	> 基于: paper_framing.md NF1-NF4, decisions D014-D017, memory.md v7 eval 发现
5	> 最后更新: 2026-05-19
6	
7	---
8	
9	## 1. 论文定位选项
10	
11	### Option A: "Why Residual Gating Fails to Recover Goal Attention in Multi-Turn Dialogue"
12	
13	- **定位**: 对 Dongre et al. (2605.12922) 诊断的首个系统性架构干预尝试 + 完整失败机制分析
14	- **核心贡献**: not "what works" but "why the most principled approach doesn't work and what this tells us about the problem's hardness"
15	- **叙事线**: Dongre 发现 goal info 在 residual stream 持续存在 (probe AUC=0.98) → 自然推论: 在 residual connection 上施加 gate 即可恢复 attention → ThreadGate 是这一推论的最直接实现 → 它的失败揭示了 NTP-GAR 梯度冲突和 attention pattern 泛化性这两个更深层的障碍
16	- **强点**: ThreadGate 的设计动机直接来自 Dongre 的 residual probe evidence，不是 ad hoc 尝试；失败机制（NF1-NF3）有完整量化；对所有基于 attention steering 的方法有警示意义
17	- **弱点**: 只有一种干预方法族（residual gating），审稿人可能质疑"你只试了一种方法"
18	- **缓解**: UG/LoRA-only 消融 + Dongre 自己的 goal re-injection 也失败 = 两种独立干预路线均失败
19	- **竞争力**: Findings ~70%，加跨模型验证有冲 main 的可能
20	
21	### Option B: "The Training-Inference Generalization Gap in Attention Steering"
22	
23	- **定位**: 发现并量化 GAR 作为训练信号的泛化性根本问题
24	- **核心贡献**: 30x training-inference GAR gap + 机制分析（NTP-GAR 梯度冲突导致 attention pattern 过拟合）
25	- **叙事线**: GAR 在训练中可被有效优化（0.02→0.48）→ 但训练学到的 attention pattern 不泛化到新对话（推理 GAR=0.016）→ 根因是 NTP loss 主导使 gate 学到反向 turn conditioning → 这意味着所有基于辅助 attention loss 的方法都面临相同泛化障碍
26	- **强点**: 泛化性问题对所有 attention steering 方法都有意义（不只是 residual gating）；30x gap 是一个 striking 数字
27	- **弱点**: 需要更多方法（不只 ThreadGate/UG）验证泛化性问题的普遍性；claim 的 scope 大于证据
28	- **缓解**: Dongre 的 goal re-injection 失败可作为间接 evidence；Base model 在 ultrachat 上 GAR≈eval GAR 排除数据分布解释
29	- **竞争力**: 如果泛化性 claim 立得住 → main track 潜力更高；但需要更强的实验支撑
30	
31	### Option C: "A Comprehensive Analysis of Goal Attention Recovery Mechanisms"
32	
33	- **定位**: 系统性比较多种 GAR 恢复策略的 survey/analysis paper
34	- **额外工作量**: 需实现 2-3 种额外干预方法（positional bias calibration, attention re-weighting, periodic goal re-injection with proper training），每种 ~10-15h GPU，时间预算不允许
35	- **竞争力**: 如果做全了很强，但 EMNLP deadline 前完不成
36	- **结论**: 不推荐，除非路线 A/B 审稿人要求 major revision 时再扩展
37	
38	### 推荐: Option A 为主，Option B 作为 framing 的一部分
39	
40	Option A 的 scope 更聚焦、证据更充分、写作负担更小。Option B 的泛化性洞察作为 Discussion section 的核心论点融入，而非作为独立 paper 的中心 claim（避免 overclaim）。
41	
42	---
43	
44	## 2. 核心消融实验（四路 eval）
45	
46	### 实验设计
47	
48	四个条件在同一组 standard_conversations.json（base model 生成，5 组 × 10 轮）上评估，消除文本 confound（NF4 修正）。
49	
50	| 条件 | 隔离目标 | GAR-ours | GAR-Dongre | nGAR | Gate profile | 状态 |
51	|------|----------|----------|-----------|------|-------------|------|
52	| Base (无训练) | 自然衰减 baseline | ~0.01 (ultrachat 已验证) | 待跑 | 待跑 | N/A | ✅ ultrachat 已跑，standard_conv 待统一 eval |
53	| TG v7 | 完整 ThreadGate | 0.016 (旧 eval) | 待重跑 | 待重跑 | 已有（D014 数据） | 待 standard_conv 统一 eval |
54	| UG | turn conditioning 增量 | 待跑 | 待跑 | 待跑 | 待跑 | ✅ 训练完成，eval 待做 |
55	| LoRA-only | gating 机制增量 | 待跑 | 待跑 | 待跑 | N/A (gate≡1.0) | 训练中 ~47.5% |
56	
57	### 关键对比
58	
59	1. **TG vs LoRA-only**: gating 机制是否有非零贡献（D012 核心问题）
60	2. **TG vs UG**: turn conditioning 是否有增量（如果两者 ≈，则 turn conditioning 无效）
61	3. **LoRA-only vs Base**: LoRA 微调本身是否改善/恶化 GAR
62	4. **所有条件 vs Base**: 任何训练干预是否改善推理时 GAR
63	
64	### 预期结果（negative result 假设下）
65	
66	- TG ≈ UG ≈ LoRA-only >> Base（LoRA 微调略改善 GAR，但 gating 无增量贡献）
67	- 或 TG ≈ UG ≈ LoRA-only ≈ Base（所有干预在推理时均无效）
68	- 任一结果都支撑 "residual gating 对推理时 GAR 无显著影响" 的 negative conclusion
69	
70	---
71	
72	## 3. 机制分析实验
73	
74	### 3.1 NTP-GAR 梯度冲突量化
75	
76	- **目的**: 量化 NTP loss 和 GAR loss 在 gate 参数上的梯度方向和幅度，为 NF1（反向 turn conditioning）提供机制解释
77	- **方法**: 在 v7 最终 checkpoint 上，取 10 个 ultrachat 样本做 single-step forward：
78	  1. 分别计算 ∇_gate(L_NTP) 和 ∇_gate(L_GAR)（28 层 × 每层 gate bias 参数）
79	  2. 逐层计算 cosine similarity: cos(∇_NTP, ∇_GAR)
80	  3. 计算梯度幅度比: ||∇_NTP|| / ||∇_GAR||
81	- **预期**: 
82	  - cosine < 0（两个梯度方向冲突），尤其在 GAR 已收敛的 late training 阶段
83	  - ||∇_NTP|| >> ||∇_GAR||（NTP 梯度幅度远大于 GAR），解释为什么 NTP 主导了 gate 学习方向
84	  - 不同 turn position 的冲突程度不同：早期 turn（短序列）冲突小，晚期 turn（长序列）NTP 梯度放大，冲突加剧
85	- **与 NF1 关系**: 如果晚期 turn 的 NTP 梯度推 gate→1.0 最强烈，直接解释为什么 gates 在晚期 turn 变为 passive（NF1 核心发现）
86	- **GPU 时间**: ~10min（10 次 forward+backward，无需训练）
87	- **代码**: 修改 train_threadgate.py，关闭 optimizer step，分别 backward 两个 loss 记录梯度
88	
89	### 3.2 Gate Profile 深入分析
90	
91	已有数据的深度挖掘，无需新实验，纯分析工作。
92	
93	#### 3.2.1 Turn-by-turn gate 值变化（已有 D014 数据）
94	- 完整 28 层 × 10 turn 的 gate heatmap
95	- 标注 NF1 发现：27/28 层 diff (turn1 - turn21) < 0
96	- 识别异常层（唯一 diff > 0 的层是哪一层？是否与 Dongre goal-encoding 层有关？）
97	
98	#### 3.2.2 Layer-wise gate 与 Dongre goal-encoding layers 对比
99	- Dongre 报告 Qwen-2.5-7B goal encoding 从 L5 开始（probe AUC 陡升）
100	- 训练时 L3/L4 为最低 gate（与 L4/L5 onset 位置 coincides，C4 suggestive）
101	- 推理时 L14/L23 动态范围最大（与训练不一致）
102	- 分析：为什么训练-推理的 critical layer 不同？是否与 NTP 梯度在不同层的相对强度有关？
103	
104	#### 3.2.3 UG vs TG gate profile 对比
105	- 如果 UG 也产生相似的 per-layer 分化模式（L3/L4 最低）→ 分化是 GAR loss 驱动的，与 turn conditioning 无关
106	- 如果 UG 分化模式不同 → turn conditioning 确实影响了哪些层被抑制（即使方向错误）
107	- 已有线索（ROADMAP.md）：UG 训练也产生与 TG 相同的 per-layer 分化模式
108	
109	### 3.3 Attention Pattern 可视化
110	
111	- **目的**: 直观展示训练前后 attention 在 goal tokens 上的实际分布，让读者"看到"问题
112	- **方法**: 
113	  1. 取 3 个 standard_conversations 样本
114	  2. 对 Base / TG / UG / LoRA-only 四个条件提取 attention weights（全层全头）
115	  3. 对每个条件：绘制 goal tokens 上的平均 attention heatmap（x 轴 = 层，y 轴 = turn）
116	  4. 对比 TG 训练时（ultrachat 数据）vs 推理时（standard_conv 数据）的 attention pattern → 可视化 30x 过拟合
117	- **预期**: 
118	  - Base: 明确的 turn-wise attention 衰减
119	  - TG/UG/LoRA-only: 与 Base 差异极小（GAR 改善不泛化）
120	  - TG 训练时: goal tokens attention 显著增强（GAR=0.48 的视觉对应）
121	- **GPU 时间**: ~20min（四个模型各 forward 一次，提取 attention weights）
122	- **图表**: 1 个 4×1 subfigure（四个条件的 attention heatmap）+ 1 个训练 vs 推理对比图
123	
124	### 3.4 GAR 泛化性分析
125	
126	- **已有**: 训练 GAR=0.48 vs 推理 GAR=0.016（NF2，30x 断层，confirmed non-bug by D015）
127	- **已有**: Base model GAR on ultrachat ≈ GAR on eval data（~0.01），排除数据分布假设
128	- **新增 per-layer GAR breakdown**:
129	  - 目的：哪些层的 GAR 过拟合最严重？是否与 gate 分化最大的层相关？
130	  - 方法：分别在训练数据和 eval 数据上计算逐层 GAR，绘制 per-layer GAR ratio (train/eval)
131	  - 预期：所有层都过拟合，但 L3/L4（gate 分化最大层）可能过拟合更严重或更轻微 — 任一结果都有分析价值
132	- **新增 training trajectory GAR 泛化曲线**:
133	  - 目的：GAR 过拟合是渐进的还是突发的？与三阶段训练动力学（plateau → phase transition → convergence）的哪个阶段对应？
134	  - 方法：在训练过程的 5 个 checkpoint（gs50, gs100, gs150, gs200, gs400）上分别计算 eval GAR
135	  - 预期：训练 GAR 在 gs75-144 急升（phase transition），eval GAR 是否同步上升？如果不同步 → 过拟合在 phase transition 阶段即开始
136	- **GPU 时间**: ~30min（5 个 checkpoint × 1 次 eval forward）
137	
138	### 3.5 训练动力学三阶段分析（已有数据，需可视化）
139	
140	v7 训练呈现清晰的三阶段动力学（memory.md 记录），这是 paper 的亮点之一：
141	
142	1. **Plateau (gs0-75)**: GAR ≈ 0.02，gate 在初始状态附近微调
143	2. **Phase transition (gs75-144)**: GAR 从 0.02 急升至 0.48，gate profile 快速分化
144	3. **Convergence (gs144+)**: GAR 稳定在 target=0.5 附近，gate_std plateau 在 0.05+
145	
146	- **可视化**: 训练 GAR + gate_mean + gate_std + L_NTP + L_GAR 的五合一 training dynamics 图
147	- **叙事价值**: 说明 MSE 双向 loss 确实能驱动 gate 学到有意义的结构，问题不在训练而在泛化
148	- **数据来源**: v7_training_dynamics_csv（已有 artifact）
149	
150	---
151	
152	## 4. 跨模型验证（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）
153	
154	跨模型验证是 Route B 从 Findings 冲 main track 的关键加分项（D017）。
155	
156	### 4.1 Phase 1 复现：GAR 衰减诊断
157	
158	- **目的**: 在第二个模型上确认 GAR 衰减现象（Dongre 已在 12 个模型上验证，但我们需要自己的数据来做完整对比）
159	- **方法**: 用 Phase 1 GAR 诊断脚本在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上跑
160	- **预期**: 同样观察到 GAR 衰减（Dongre 在 Llama-3.1-8B 上报告 27-48% 衰减范围）
161	- **代码适配**: turn_utils.py 已支持 Llama chat template（D015 后完成）
162	- **模型路径**: /root/.cache/modelscope/hub/models/LLM-Research/Meta-Llama-3___1-8B-Instruct/（ModelScope 通道，已有 tokenizer，权重 ~16GB 需下载）
163	- **GPU 时间**: ~30min
164	- **磁盘**: 需先清理 Qwen cache 腾出空间（当前 15G/30G 可用，Llama 权重 ~16GB）
165	
166	### 4.2 ThreadGate v7 训练
167	
168	- **目的**: 在 Llama 上复现 ThreadGate 的 negative finding
169	- **方法**: 用与 Qwen v7 相同的设置（MSE+gate_div, λ_gar=1.0, λ_div=1.0, 3 epoch）
170	- **预期**: 
171	  - 训练 GAR 同样可被优化到 ~0.5（MSE loss 有效性是模型无关的）
172	  - 推理 GAR 同样过拟合（如果确认 → NF2 普遍性加强）
173	  - Turn conditioning 同样反向（如果确认 → NF1 是 NTP-GAR 竞争的结构性后果，不是 Qwen 特有的）
174	- **代码适配**: threadgate.py 自动读取 hidden_dim (4096 for Llama vs 3584 for Qwen) / num_layers (32 for Llama vs 28 for Qwen)
175	- **GPU 时间**: ~6h（Llama-3.1-8B 比 Qwen-2.5-7B 稍大）
176	
177	### 4.3 消融（可选，视时间预算）
178	
179	- 如果时间允许：在 Llama 上也跑 UG + LoRA-only 消融
180	- 如果时间不允许：只做 TG + Base 对比，消融数据仅在 Qwen 上报告
181	- GPU 时间（如做）: UG ~6h + LoRA-only ~6h
182	
183	### 4.4 跨模型 gate profile 对比
184	
185	- 如果 Llama 上也出现类似的 per-layer 分化模式（某些浅层 gate 最低）→ 更强的普遍性 evidence
186	- 如果 Llama 的 critical layer 不同（如 L2 最低，对应 Dongre 报告 Llama goal-encoding onset 在 L2）→ 说明 gate 确实学到了模型特异的 goal-encoding 结构，但仍然无法泛化到推理
187	- 两种结果都有分析价值
188	
189	---
190	
191	## 5. 与 Dongre et al. (2605.12922) 的关系
192	
193	### 互补定位
194	
195	| 维度 | Dongre et al. | 本文 |
196	|------|---------------|------|
197	| 核心问题 | "发生了什么" + "为什么" | "怎么干预" + "为什么干预失败" |
198	| GAR 衰减 | 定义 GAR，12 模型量化衰减 | 独立确认（Qwen 70.8% raw, 21% normalized），我们的测量定义更宽（含 first user query） |
199	| 机制分析 | residual probe AUC=0.98（goal info 持续存在） | NTP-GAR 梯度冲突 + 30x 泛化 gap + turn conditioning 反转 |
200	| 干预尝试 | goal re-injection (Appendix H, 失败) | ThreadGate residual gating (失败) + UG/LoRA-only 消融 |
201	| 结论 | attention 通道关闭但 residual stream 保留 goal info | 即使在 residual stream 上施加 principled gating，NTP 梯度压制使 gate 无法学到正确的 turn-selective 干预 |
202	
203	### 共同推论
204	
205	两种独立干预路线（prompt-level goal re-injection + architecture-level residual gating）均失败，共同指向：
206	
207	1. 简单的 attention steering 不足以对抗 NTP 的优化压力
208	2. 需要更深层的架构改变（如 decoupled goal-processing pathway，或 inference-time attention intervention 绕过训练泛化问题）
209	
210	### 我们的独特增量
211	
212	Dongre 诊断了问题，但没有解释为什么干预难以成功。我们量化了三个具体障碍：
213	1. **NTP-GAR 梯度冲突**（NF1 + 3.1 实验）: NTP loss 在 gate 参数上的梯度方向与 GAR loss 相反
214	2. **Attention pattern 不泛化**（NF2 + 3.4 实验）: 训练中学到的 attention redistribution 不迁移到新对话
215	3. **Gate 干预强度不足**（NF3）: 推理时 gate 仅 2-7% attenuation，远不足以改变 attention pattern
216	
217	---
218	
219	## 6. 论文结构草案（Option A framing）
220	
221	1. **Abstract** (150-250 words): 问题（GAR 衰减）→ 动机（Dongre residual probe → residual gating 是自然方案）→ 方法（ThreadGate）→ 核心发现（三个失败机制）→ 意义
222	2. **Introduction** (5 段): 多轮对话退化 → Dongre 诊断 + residual stream 持续性 → ThreadGate 设计动机 → 三个 negative findings 概述 → 贡献列表
223	3. **Related Work**: multi-turn degradation / residual gating & adaptive depth / attention steering
224	4. **Background**: GAR 定义（cite Dongre）+ residual stream persistence 证据
225	5. **Method**: ThreadGate 架构 + MSE+gate_div loss + 训练设置
226	6. **Experiments**: 四路消融 + 跨模型验证 + 训练动力学
227	7. **Analysis**: NTP-GAR 梯度冲突 + GAR 泛化性分析 + gate profile 深入分析 + attention 可视化
228	8. **Discussion**: 为什么 residual gating 不够 + 对 attention steering 方法的启示 + 潜在解决方向
229	9. **Conclusion**: negative result 总结 + 对社区的建议
230	
231	### 贡献列表（草案）
232	
233	1. 首个基于 residual stream persistence 证据设计的架构级 GAR 恢复干预（ThreadGate），及其完整失败分析
234	2. 发现并量化 NTP-GAR 梯度冲突导致 turn conditioning 反向学习的机制
235	3. 量化 GAR 训练信号的泛化性问题（30x training-inference gap），对所有基于辅助 attention loss 的方法有警示意义
236	4. 四路消融 + 跨模型验证的完整实验设计，为后续研究提供 baseline 和诊断工具
237

需要更多方法（不只 ThreadGate/UG）验证泛化性问题的普遍性；claim 的 scope 大于证据\n- **缓解**: Dongre 的 goal re-injection 失败可作为间接 evidence；Base model 在 ultrachat 上 GAR≈eval GAR 排除数据分布解释\n- **竞争力**: 如果泛化性 claim 立得住 → main track eval）\n\n### 实验设计\n\n四个条件在同一组 standard_conversations.json（base model 生成，5 组 × 10 轮）上评估，消除文本 confound（NF4 修正）。\n\n| 条件 | 隔离目标 | GAR-ours | GAR-Dongre | nGAR | Gate profile | 状态 |\n|------|----------|----------|-----------|------|-------------|------|\n| Base (无训练) | 自然衰减 baseline | ~0.01 (ultrachat 已验证) | 待跑 | 待跑 | N/A | ✅ ultrachat 已跑，standard_conv 待统一 eval |\n| TG v7 | 完整 ThreadGate | 0.016 (旧 eval) | 待重跑 | 待重跑 | 已有（D014 数据） | 待 standard_conv 统一 eval |\n| UG | turn conditioning 增量 | 待跑 | 待跑 | 待跑 | 待跑 | ✅ 训练完成，eval 待做 |\n| LoRA-only | gating 机制增量 | 待跑 | 待跑 | 待跑 | N/A (gate≡1.0) | 训练中 ~47.5% |\n\n### 关键对比\n\n1. **TG vs LoRA-only**: gating 机制是否有非零贡献（D012 核心问题）\n2. **TG vs UG**: turn conditioning 是否有增量（如果两者 ≈，则 turn conditioning 无效）\n3. **LoRA-only vs Base**: LoRA 微调本身是否改善/恶化 GAR\n4. **所有条件 vs Base**: 任何训练干预是否改善推理时 GAR\n\n### 预期结果（negative result 假设下）\n\n- TG ≈ UG ≈ LoRA-only >> Base（LoRA 微调略改善 GAR，但 gating 无增量贡献）\n- 或 TG ≈ UG ≈ LoRA-only ≈ Base（所有干预在推理时均无效）\n- 任一结果都支撑 \"residual gating 对推理时 GAR 无显著影响\" 的 negative conclusion\n\n---\n\n## 3. 机制分析实验\n\n### 3.1 NTP-GAR 梯度冲突量化\n\n- **目的**: 量化 NTP loss 和 GAR loss 在 gate 参数上的梯度方向和幅度，为 NF1（反向 turn conditioning）提供机制解释\n- **方法**: 在 v7 最终 checkpoint 上，取 10 个 ultrachat 样本做 single-step forward：\n  1. 分别计算 ∇_gate(L_NTP) 和 ∇_gate(L_GAR)（28 层 × 每层 gate bias 参数）\n  2. 逐层计算 cosine similarity: cos(∇_NTP, ∇_GAR)\n  3. 计算梯度幅度比: ||∇_NTP|| / ||∇_GAR||\n- **预期**: \n  - cosine < 0（两个梯度方向冲突），尤其在 GAR 已收敛的 late training 阶段\n  - ||∇_NTP|| >> ||∇_GAR||（NTP 梯度幅度远大于 GAR），解释为什么 NTP 主导了 gate 学习方向\n  - 不同 turn position 的冲突程度不同：早期 turn（短序列）冲突小，晚期 turn（长序列）NTP 梯度放大，冲突加剧\n- **与 NF1 关系**: 如果晚期 turn 的 NTP 梯度推 gate→1.0 最强烈，直接解释为什么 gates 在晚期 turn 变为 passive（NF1 核心发现）\n- **GPU 时间**: ~10min（10 次 forward+backward，无需训练）\n- **代码**: 修改 train_threadgate.py，关闭 optimizer step，分别 backward 两个 loss 记录梯度\n\n### 3.2 Gate Profile 深入分析\n\n已有数据的深度挖掘，无需新实验，纯分析工作。\n\n#### 3.2.1 Turn-by-turn gate 值变化（已有 D014 数据）\n- 完整 28 层 × 10 turn 的 gate heatmap\n- 标注 NF1 发现：27/28 层 diff (turn1 - turn21) < 0\n- 识别异常层（唯一 diff > 0 的层是哪一层？是否与 Dongre goal-encoding 层有关？）\n\n#### 3.2.2 Layer-wise gate 与 Dongre goal-encoding layers 对比\n- Dongre 报告 Qwen-2.5-7B goal encoding 从 L5 开始（probe AUC 陡升）\n- 训练时 L3/L4 为最低 gate（与 L4/L5 onset 位置 coincides，C4 suggestive）\n- 推理时 L14/L23 动态范围最大（与训练不一致）\n- 分析：为什么训练-推理的 critical layer 不同？是否与 NTP 梯度在不同层的相对强度有关？\n\n#### 3.2.3 UG vs TG gate profile 对比\n- 如果 UG 也产生相似的 per-layer 分化模式（L3/L4 最低）→ 分化是 GAR loss 驱动的，与 turn conditioning 无关\n- 如果 UG 分化模式不同 → turn conditioning 确实影响了哪些层被抑制（即使方向错误）\n- 已有线索（ROADMAP.md）：UG 训练也产生与 TG 相同的 per-layer 分化模式\n\n### 3.3 Attention Pattern 可视化\n\n- **目的**: 直观展示训练前后 attention 在 goal tokens 上的实际分布，让读者\"看到\"问题\n- **方法**: \n  1. 取 3 个 standard_conversations 样本\n  2. 对 Base / TG / UG / LoRA-only 四个条件提取 attention weights（全层全头）\n  3. 对每个条件：绘制 goal tokens 上的平均 attention heatmap（x 轴 = 层，y 轴 = turn）\n  4. 对比 TG 训练时（ultrachat 数据）vs 推理时（standard_conv 数据）的 attention pattern → 可视化 30x 过拟合\n- **预期**: \n  - Base: 明确的 turn-wise attention 衰减\n  - TG/UG/LoRA-only: 与 Base 差异极小（GAR 改善不泛化）\n  - TG 训练时: goal tokens attention 显著增强（GAR=0.48 的视觉对应）\n- **GPU 时间**: ~20min（四个模型各 forward 一次，提取 attention weights）\n- **图表**: 1 个 4×1 subfigure（四个条件的 attention heatmap）+ 1 个训练 vs 推理对比图\n\n### 3.4 GAR 泛化性分析\n\n- **已有**: 训练 GAR=0.48 vs 推理 GAR=0.016（NF2，30x 断层，confirmed non-bug by D015）\n- **已有**: Base model GAR on ultrachat ≈ GAR on eval data（~0.01），排除数据分布假设\n- **新增 per-layer GAR breakdown**:\n  - 目的：哪些层的 GAR 过拟合最严重？是否与 gate 分化最大的层相关？\n  - 方法：分别在训练数据和 eval 数据上计算逐层 GAR，绘制 per-layer GAR ratio (train/eval)\n  - 预期：所有层都过拟合，但 L3/L4（gate 分化最大层）可能过拟合更严重或更轻微 — 任一结果都有分析价值\n- **新增 training trajectory GAR 泛化曲线**:\n  - 目的：GAR 过拟合是渐进的还是突发的？与三阶段训练动力学（plateau → phase transition → convergence）的哪个阶段对应？\n  - 方法：在训练过程的 5 个 checkpoint（gs50, gs100, gs150, gs200, gs400）上分别计算 eval GAR\n  - 预期：训练 GAR 在 gs75-144 急升（phase transition），eval GAR 是否同步上升？如果不同步 → 过拟合在 phase transition 阶段即开始\n- **GPU 时间**: ~30min（5 个 checkpoint × 1 次 eval forward）\n\n### 3.5 训练动力学三阶段分析（已有数据，需可视化）\n\nv7 训练呈现清晰的三阶段动力学（memory.md 记录），这是 paper 的亮点之一：\n\n1. **Plateau (gs0-75)**: GAR ≈ 0.02，gate 在初始状态附近微调\n2. **Phase transition (gs75-144)**: GAR 从 0.02 急升至 0.48，gate profile 快速分化\n3. **Convergence (gs144+)**: GAR 稳定在 target=0.5 附近，gate_std plateau 在 0.05+\n\n- **可视化**: 训练 GAR + gate_mean + gate_std + L_NTP + L_GAR 的五合一 training dynamics 图\n- **叙事价值**: 说明 MSE 双向 loss 确实能驱动 gate 学到有意义的结构，问题不在训练而在泛化\n- **数据来源**: v7_training_dynamics_csv（已有 artifact）\n\n---\n\n## 4. 跨模型验证（Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）\n\n跨模型验证是 Route B 从 Findings 冲 main track 的关键加分项（D017）。\n\n### 4.1 Phase 1 复现：GAR 衰减诊断\n\n- **目的**: 在第二个模型上确认 GAR 衰减现象（Dongre 已在 12 个模型上验证，但我们需要自己的数据来做完整对比）\n- **方法**: 用 Phase 1 GAR 诊断脚本在 Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct 上跑\n- **预期**: 同样观察到 GAR 衰减（Dongre 在 Llama-3.1-8B 上报告 27-48% 衰减范围）\n- **代码适配**: turn_utils.py 已支持 Llama chat template（D015 后完成）\n- **模型路径**: /root/.cache/modelscope/hub/models/LLM-Research/Meta-Llama-3___1-8B-Instruct/（ModelScope 通道，已有 tokenizer，权重 ~16GB 需下载）\n- **GPU 时间**: ~30min\n- **磁盘**: 需先清理 Qwen cache 腾出空间（当前 15G/30G 可用，Llama 权重 ~16GB）\n\n### 4.2 ThreadGate v7 训练\n\n- **目的**: 在 Llama 上复现 ThreadGate 的 negative finding\n- **方法**: 用与 Qwen v7 相同的设置（MSE+gate_div, λ_gar=1.0, λ_div=1.0, 3 epoch）\n- **预期**: \n  - 训练 GAR 同样可被优化到 ~0.5（MSE loss 有效性是模型无关的）\n  - 推理 GAR 同样过拟合（如果确认 → NF2 普遍性加强）\n  - Turn conditioning 同样反向（如果确认 → NF1 是 NTP-GAR 竞争的结构性后果，不是 Qwen 特有的）\n- **代码适配**: threadgate.py 自动读取 hidden_dim (4096 for Llama vs 3584 for Qwen) / num_layers (32 for Llama vs 28 for Qwen)\n- **GPU 时间**: ~6h（Llama-3.1-8B 比 Qwen-2.5-7B 稍大）\n\n### 4.3 消融（可选，视时间预算）\n\n- 如果时间允许：在 Llama 上也跑 UG + LoRA-only 消融\n- 如果时间不允许：只做 TG + Base 对比，消融数据仅在 Qwen 上报告\n- GPU 时间（如做）: UG ~6h + LoRA-only ~6h\n\n### 4.4 跨模型 gate profile 对比\n\n- 如果 Llama 上也出现类似的 per-layer 分化模式（某些浅层 gate 最低）→ 更强的普遍性 evidence\n- 如果 Llama 的 critical layer 不同（如 L2 最低，对应 Dongre 报告 Llama goal-encoding onset 在 L2）→ 说明 gate 确实学到了模型特异的 goal-encoding 结构，但仍然无法泛化到推理\n- 两种结果都有分析价值\n\n---\n\n## 5. 与 Dongre et al. (2605.12922) 的关系\n\n### 互补定位\n\n| 维度 | Dongre et al. | 本文 |\n|------|---------------|------|\n| 核心问题 | \"发生了什么\" + \"为什么\" | \"怎么干预\" + \"为什么干预失败\" |\n| GAR 衰减 | 定义 GAR，12 模型量化衰减 | 独立确认（Qwen 70.8% raw, 21% normalized），我们的测量定义更宽（含 first user query） |\n| 机制分析 | residual probe AUC=0.98（goal info 持续存在） | NTP-GAR 梯度冲突 + 30x 泛化 gap + turn conditioning 反转 |\n| 干预尝试 | goal re-injection (Appendix H, 失败) | ThreadGate residual gating (失败) + UG/LoRA-only 消融 |\n| 结论 | attention 通道关闭但 residual stream 保留 goal info | 即使在 residual stream 上施加 principled gating，NTP 梯度压制使 gate 无法学到正确的 turn-selective 干预 |\n\n### 共同推论\n\n两种独立干预路线（prompt-level goal re-injection + architecture-level residual gating）均失败，共同指向：\n\n1. 简单的 attention steering 不足以对抗 NTP 的优化压力\n2. 需要更深层的架构改变（如 decoupled goal-processing pathway，或 inference-time attention intervention 绕过训练泛化问题）\n\n### 我们的独特增量\n\nDongre 诊断了问题，但没有解释为什么干预难以成功。我们量化了三个具体障碍：\n1. **NTP-GAR 梯度冲突**（NF1 + 3.1 实验）: NTP loss 在 gate 参数上的梯度方向与 GAR loss 相反\n2. **Attention pattern 不泛化**（NF2 + 3.4 实验）: 训练中学到的 attention redistribution 不迁移到新对话\n3. **Gate 干预强度不足**（NF3）: 推理时 gate 仅 2-7% attenuation，远不足以改变 attention pattern\n\n---\n\n## 6. 论文结构草案（Option A framing）\n\n1. **Abstract** (150-250 words): 问题（GAR 衰减）→ 动机（Dongre residual probe → residual gating 是自然方案）→ 方法（ThreadGate）→ 核心发现（三个失败机制）→ 意义\n2. **Introduction** (5 段): 多轮对话退化 → Dongre 诊断 + residual stream 持续性 → ThreadGate 设计动机 → 三个 negative findings 概述 → 贡献列表\n3. **Related Work**: multi-turn degradation / residual gating & adaptive depth / attention steering\n4. **Background**: GAR 定义（cite Dongre）+ residual stream persistence 证据\n5. **Method**: ThreadGate 架构 + MSE+gate_div loss + 训练设置\n6. **Experiments**: 四路消融 + 跨模型验证 + 训练动力学\n7. **Analysis**: NTP-GAR 梯度冲突 + GAR 泛化性分析 + gate profile 深入分析 + attention 可视化\n8. **Discussion**: 为什么 residual gating 不够 + 对 attention steering 方法的启示 + 潜在解决方向\n9. **Conclusion**: negative result 总结 + 对社区的建议\n\n### 贡献列表（草案）\n\n1. 首个基于 residual stream persistence 证据设计的架构级 GAR 恢复干预（ThreadGate），及其完整失败分析\n2. 发现并量化 NTP-GAR 梯度冲突导致 turn conditioning 反向学习的机制\n3. 量化 GAR 训练信号的泛化性问题（30x training-inference gap），对所有基于辅助 attention loss 的方法有警示意义\n4. 四路消融 + 跨模型验证的完整实验设计，为后续研究提供 baseline 和诊断工具\n\n---\n\n## 7. 实验时间表\n\n| 实验 | GPU 时间 | 依赖 | 预计完成 |\n|------|----------|------|----------|\n| LoRA-only 训练完成 | ~3h (剩余) | 无 | D+0 (2026-05-19) |\n| 四路 eval on standard_conv (Qwen) | ~1h | LoRA-only 完成 | D+0 |\n| 梯度冲突分析 (3.1) | ~10min | v7 checkpoint | D+0 |\n| Attention 可视化 (3.3) | ~20min | 四路 checkpoint | D+0 |\n| GAR 泛化曲线 — 5 checkpoints (3.4) | ~30min | v7 中间 checkpoints | D+1 |\n| 训练动力学可视化 (3.5) | ~0 (纯绘图) | v7_training_dynamics_csv | D+0 |\n| Gate profile 深入分析 (3.2) | ~0 (纯分析) | 四路 eval 数据 | D+1 |\n| Llama 模型下载 | ~30min (网络) | 磁盘清理 | D+1 |\n| Phase 1 GAR 诊断 (Llama) | ~30min | Llama 模型下载 | D+1 |\n| TG v7 训练 (Llama) | ~6h | Llama 模型下载 | D+2 |\n| Llama eval + gate profile | ~1h | Llama TG 完成 | D+2 |\n| [可选] Llama UG + LoRA-only | ~12h | Llama TG eval | D+3-4 |\n\n**总 GPU 时间**: ~12h（核心）+ ~12h（可选 Llama 消融）\n**写作启动**: D+2（Qwen 全部数据就绪后可开始 Method/Background/Related Work）\n**完整初稿**: D+7-10（假设跨模型验证顺利）\n\n---\n\n## 8. 风险与应急\n\n| 风险 | 概率 | 影响 | 应急 |\n|------|------|------|------|\n| LoRA-only 意外优于 TG（gating 有负面贡献） | 低 | 叙事调整：gating 不仅无效还有害（更强的 negative finding） | 额外分析 why gating hurts |\n| Llama 上 GAR 衰减模式与 Qwen 显著不同 | 中 | 跨模型 claim 弱化 | 聚焦 Qwen 深度分析，Llama 作为补充 |\n| Llama 上 ThreadGate 意外成功 | 极低 | 整个 negative framing 需要重写 | 转回 Route A，Qwen 失败 + Llama 成功本身是更有趣的故事 |\n| 磁盘空间不足以同时存 Qwen + Llama checkpoints | 中 | Llama 实验延迟 | 先完成 Qwen 所有分析，删除中间 checkpoints，再下载 Llama |\n| 审稿人认为\"只测了 residual gating 一种方法，negative result 太窄\" | 高 | reject 风险 | Discussion 中系统性分析为什么其他方法（attention re-weighting, positional bias）也面临类似障碍；cite Dongre goal <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Key findings:

1. **v7 checkpoints**: Only epoch-level checkpoints (epoch_0 = gs1000, epoch_1 = gs2000, epoch_2 = gs3000) + final. **No intermediate checkpoints at gs50/100/150/200/400** as required by Section 3.4 (GAR generalization curve). This means the experiment in 3.4 that requires 5 checkpoints at gs50-400 cannot be executed as designed. Only epoch_0 (gs1000) is close-ish, but it's after the phase transition (gs75-144) is already complete.


2. **Eval data mismatch**: The design doc specifies using standard_conversations.json for the four-way eval, but the actual script uses ultrachat_eval_sample.json instead—a 50-conversation dataset versus the 5-conversation standard set. This discrepancy needs resolution to determine which evaluation set was actually intended.

3. **Trade-offs between datasets**: Using the larger ultrachat sample provides stronger statistical power, while sticking with standard_conversations maintains consistency with v7's previous evaluation and allows direct comparison. Running both evaluations would give the most comprehensive validation.
```

{
  "text": "[severity: warning] Route B 实验设计审查 — 总体合理，3 个问题需修正\n\n## 总体评估\n\n设计文档质量高：Option A/B 定位对比清晰、Dongre 互补关系准确、风险表全面。推荐 Option A 为主 + Option B 融入 Discussion 的策略合理。\n\n## 具体问题\n\n### 1. [severity: warning] 四路 eval 数据源不一致\n\n设计文档 §2 写 \"standard_conversations.json（5 组 × 10 轮）\"，但已完成的 `run_4way_eval.sh` 脚本实际使用 `ultrachat_eval_sample.json`（2737 行，~50 conversations）。\n\n**影响**：\n- standard_conversations: 可与 v7 先前 eval 直接对比，但统计力弱（n=5）\n- ultrachat_eval_sample: 统计更稳健（n=50），但与 v7 先前数据不可直接比较\n\n**建议**：两组都跑。ultrachat 作为主表（统计力），standard_conversations 作为 appendix 交叉验证 + 与 v7 旧 eval 的一致性检查。eval 脚本需补充 standard_conversations 版本。\n\n### 2. [severity: warning] §3.4 GAR 泛化曲线需要的中间 checkpoint 不存在\n\n§3.4 设计需要 v7 的 gs50/100/150/200/400 checkpoint 来追踪 eval GAR 在训练过程中的变化。但 v7 实际只保存了 epoch 级 checkpoint：\n- `epoch_0/gate_params.pt` (gs1000)\n- `epoch_1/gate_params.pt` (gs2000)\n- `epoch_2/gate_params.pt` (gs3000)\n- `final/gate_params.pt` (gs3000)\n\nPhase transition 发生在 gs75-144，但最早可用的 checkpoint 是 gs1000（远在收敛之后）。**无法观察 phase transition 阶段的 eval GAR 变化。**\n\n**选项**：\n- A: 接受限制，用 epoch_0/1/2 做粗粒度泛化曲线（3 个点而非 5 个），仍有信息价值（显示收敛后 eval GAR 是否随训练推进变化）\n- B: 重新跑一次短训练（~2h），专门在 gs50/100/150/200/300/500/1000 保存 checkpoint，代价 2 GPU·h\n- 推荐 B，因为 phase transition 阶段的 eval GAR 变化是 §3.4 的核心叙事点\n\n### 3. [severity: info] 缺少 NTP perplexity 对比\n\n设计中未包含各模型的 held-out PPL 对比。PPL 能回答 \"gating 是否损害语言建模质量\"：\n- 如果 TG PPL > LoRA-only PPL → gating 有害（更强的 negative finding）\n- 如果 TG PPL ≈ LoRA-only PPL → gating 中性（冗余但无害）\n\n这个数据获取成本极低（eval 时已做 forward pass，只需多记录一个指标），建议加入。\n\n## 无问题项\n\n- Option A framing 对标 Dongre 的定位准确，\"why the most principled approach doesn't work\" 叙事有说服力\n- §3.1 梯度冲突实验设计干净，cosine similarity + magnitude ratio 能直接支撑 NF1\n- §5 Dongre 互补表准确，\"两种独立干预路线均失败\" 的论点有效\n- §8 风险表全面，\"审稿人嫌方法太窄\" 确实是最高概率风险\n- 贡献列表 4 条合理，强度匹配 Findings 级别\n- 时间表 D+7-10 完稿在 ~10 weeks deadline 内充裕",

[WRITE HANDOFF] 请立即写你的 handoff。

**格式硬约束**:
- 整条回复**只能**是 &lt;handoff>...&lt;/handoff> 标签包裹
- 禁止在标签外写任何寒暄 / 说明 / 确认（"好的"/"我开始写"/"写完了"等）

**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。
  下一个"你"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

示例格式:
&lt;handoff>
### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 cuda:4 / cuda:5（之前两次违规 cuda:0 被 Director CRITICAL）
- submit_training_job 不支持 shell builtin（source/conda activate 全不行）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-8x6000，工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/hybrid_reasoning_phases
- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python（绝对路径，不走 conda activate）
- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/qwen35_4b_direct
- 活跃：pilot_011 (PID 203221, tmux=pilot_011, cuda:4)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
我刚完成 pilot_010 分析，正在等 pilot_011 (Falcon-H1R) CoT 生成完成（6/50）。
registry: pilot_011 RUNNING；其他全 DONE。

### 4. 📋 待办
- pilot_011 cron 回调 → spawn worker 跑 probe + GC4 计算
- GC4 ≥2pp → 转架构 narrative；<2pp → null-result paper outline 已就绪

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
Falcon-H1R 是最后一个数据点。已知 4B/9B GC4 都 FAIL。
易错：不要把 GC4-par (Falcon parallel) 和 GC4-seq (Qwen sequential) 直接对比。
&lt;/handoff>
